### Performance Improvements

- **basemaps:** use parquet-wasm for zero-conversion native GeoArrow loading ([#74](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/issues/74)) ([30c7074](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/30c707463328c3fcc704e6bde135849e44a9b3fd))

### Performance Improvements

- **basemaps:** use parquet-wasm for zero-conversion native GeoArrow loading ([#74](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/issues/74)) ([30c7074](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/30c707463328c3fcc704e6bde135849e44a9b3fd))
- **classification:** add memoization cache for breaks results ([138f38b](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/138f38be11ba506be1638e5bfbd09cfc601aaf67))
- **data-pipeline:** share file head read between detectors, pre-filter geo columns ([72d53ab](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/72d53abe4ad97da048eb343f7ffede5ffea1e4b7))
- **duckdb/arrow:** use streaming query for geometry table fetches ([47cf0f8](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/47cf0f8762ee9710ae713c71e41850e093b80c59))
- **duckdb:** batch column operations with single deferred re-analysis ([91627c0](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/91627c06c91af335cdf36df81e3f2de44a824175))
- **duckdb:** defer arrow metadata and streamline table reads ([20e2389](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/20e238939cfcfcc639f95f01a84f3083bee0c381))
- **duckdb:** optimize arrow ops with geom column prefetch and search sample columns ([b749bc8](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/b749bc8b78948634aef6b348052244b80e952b39))
- **join:** optimisation suggestion basemap — exact match + déduplication + parquet corrigé ([#101](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/issues/101)) ([68523f6](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/68523f6de26f84e9042e608b80d052db672adaf8))
- **lighthouse:** 93→98 perf — lazy-load map chunk + font preconnect ([2ccda68](https://github.com/AtelierCartographie/khartis-v3/commit/2ccda68d588d0b3537e378e402aa0f337403dcab))
